\frac {\left| \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} \left[ \sum_ {j = 1} [ y _ {j} - m _ {j} ] \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right] \right| \right| _ {2}}{(c + m _ {4}) n \sqrt {n} m _ {1}} \leq
$$

$$
\sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \left| \left| [ k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} [ k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \right| \right| _ {f} \leq
$$

$$
\sqrt {n} \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \left| \left| [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \right| \right| _ {2}
$$

and using the properties of the matrix 2-norm  $||\cdot ||_2$

$$
\left| \left| [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \right| \right| _ {2} \leq \frac {1}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}}
$$

hence,

$$
\frac {\left| \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} \left[ \sum_ {j = 1} [ y _ {j} - m _ {j} ] \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \left[ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i j} \right] \right] \right| \right| _ {2}}{(c + m _ {4}) n ^ {2} m _ {1}} \leq \tag {11}
$$

$$
\sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \left| \left| [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \right| \right| _ {2} \left| \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right| \right| _ {2} \left| \left| [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \right| \right| _ {2} \leq \frac {1}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}} \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \left| \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right| \right| _ {2}.
$$

let us study the last term in the expression above. using  $\sqrt{c_1^2 + \ldots + c_r^2} \leq |c_1| + \ldots + |c_r|$  for any set of real numbers  $c_1, \ldots, c_r \in \mathbb{r}$  we have

$$
\left| \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right| \right| _ {2} = \left| \left| \left[ \begin{array}{c c c c} \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {1}), & \ldots & \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {n}) \\ \vdots & \vdots & \vdots & l \\ \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {n}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {1}), & \ldots & \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {n}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {n}) \end{array} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} \leq
$$

$$
\left| \left| \left[ \begin{array}{c c c} \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {1}), & \ldots & \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {n}) \\ \vdots & \vdots & \vdots \\ \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {n}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {1}), & \ldots & \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {n}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {n}) \end{array} \right] \right| \right| _ {f} =
$$

$$
\sqrt {\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left[ \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x} _ {i} , \pmb {x} _ {j}) \right] ^ {2}} \leq \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x} _ {i}, \pmb {x} _ {j}) \right|.
$$

substituting this expression in (11) gives us

$$
\frac {\left| \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} \left[ \sum_ {j = 1} [ y _ {j} - m _ {j} ] \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \left[ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i j} \right] \right] \right| \right| _ {2}}{(c + m _ {4}) n ^ {2} m _ {1}} \leq \frac {1}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}} \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {d} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} _ {i}, \boldsymbol {x} _ {j}) \right| \leq \tag {12}
$$

$$
\frac {\sqrt {p}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} | | \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} k _ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x} _ {i}, \pmb {x} _ {j}) | | _ {2} \leq \frac {n ^ {2} \sqrt {p} m _ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}}.
$$

hence, combining results (9) and (12) we have

$$
\left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \mu_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} | \mathcal {d}) \right| \right| _ {2} \leq \frac {(c + m _ {4}) n ^ {2} m _ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}} \left[ 1 + \frac {n ^ {2} m _ {1} \sqrt {p}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}} \right]. \tag {13}
$$

now, let us focus on the gradient of the posterior standard deviation,

$$
\nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \sigma_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} | \mathcal {d}) = \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \sqrt {k _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} , \boldsymbol {x}) - \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ^ {\mathsf {t}} [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} ^ {2} \mathbf {i} ] ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}}} \right] =
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 \sigma_ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x} | \mathcal {d})} \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} \left[ k _ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x}, \pmb {x}) - \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ^ {\top} [ \pmb {k} _ {\pmb {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} ^ {2} \mathbf {i} ] ^ {- 1} \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} \right] = \frac {1}{2 \sigma_ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x})} \left[ \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} k _ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x}, \pmb {x}) - \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} \left[ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ^ {\top} [ \pmb {k} _ {\pmb {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} ^ {2} \mathbf {i} ] ^ {- 1} \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} \right] \right].
$$

let us study the second gradient expression. using our notation we have

$$
\boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ^ {\mathsf {t}} [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} ^ {2} \mathbf {i} ] ^ {- 1} \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} = \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ^ {\mathsf {t}} \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j}.
$$

hence, for the gradient,

$$
\nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ^ {\mathsf {t}} \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] =
$$

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} \right] [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} \right] [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} +
$$

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} \left[ [ \pmb {b} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {j}.
$$

and for the norm of the above expression we have

$$
\left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ^ {\mathsf {t}} \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] =
$$

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} | [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} | + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} | | \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} | | _ {2} \left| [ [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} ] [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right| +
$$

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} | | \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} [ [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} ] | | _ {2} \left| [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right|.
$$

let us now bound each term in this expression:

1. the first term:

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} | [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {j} | \leq
$$

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} | | \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} | | _ {2} | | \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} | | _ {2} \leq m _ {1} ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ [ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right| \right| _ {2}
$$

using the previous bound for  $\left\| \nabla_{\pmb{\theta}}\left[[\pmb{b}_{\pmb{\theta}}]_{ij}\right]\right\| _2$  we have:

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} \left[ [ \pmb {b} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} | [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {j} | \leq m _ {1} ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} \left[ \pmb {k} _ {\pmb {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} ^ {2} \mathbf {i} \right] _ {i j} ^ {- 1} \right| =
$$

$$
n m _ {1} ^ {2} \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \left| \left| [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} [ \boldsymbol {k} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \boldsymbol {i} ] ^ {- 1} \right| \right| _ {f} \leq
$$

$$
n ^ {\frac {3}{2}} m _ {1} ^ {2} \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \left| \left| [ \pmb {k} _ {\pmb {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \pmb {i} ] ^ {- 1} \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} \pmb {k} _ {\pmb {\theta}} [ \pmb {k} _ {\pmb {\theta}} + \sigma_ {n} \pmb {i} ] ^ {- 1} \right| \right| _ {2}
$$

since  $\left|\left|\left[\pmb{k}_{\pmb{\theta}} + \sigma_n\pmb{i}\right]^{-1}\right|\right|_2 \leq \frac{1}{\sigma_n^2}$  we have:

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} \left[ [ \pmb {b} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} | [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {j} | \leq \frac {n \sqrt {n} m _ {1} ^ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}} \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {p} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {r}} k _ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x} _ {i}, \pmb {x} _ {j}) \right|.
$$

using  $\sum_{r=1}^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta_r}k_\pmb{\theta}(\pmb{x}_i,\pmb{x}_j)\right| = \sqrt{p}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\nabla_\pmb{\theta}k_\pmb{\theta}(\pmb{x}_i,\pmb{x}_j)\|_2 \leq n^2\sqrt{p}m_2$ , we have:

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\pmb {\theta}} \left[ [ \pmb {b} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i j} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} | [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {i} [ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta}} ] _ {j} | \leq \frac {n ^ {\frac {7}{2}} \sqrt {p} m _ {1} ^ {2} m _ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}}
$$

2. the second and the third terms are identical with respect to the bounding strategy,

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} | | \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i} \right] | | _ {2} \left| \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {j} \left[ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i j} \right| = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left| \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (i,:) \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right| | | \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i} \right] | | _ {2} \leq
$$

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (i,:) \right| \right| _ {2} \left| \left| \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right| \right| _ {2} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} \leq \left| \left| \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right| \right| _ {f} \left| \left| \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right| \right| _ {2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i} \right] \right| \right| _ {2},
$$

since  $||\pmb{b}_{\pmb{\theta}}||_{f} \leq \sqrt{\mathrm{rank}(\pmb{b}_{\pmb{\theta}})} ||\pmb{b}_{\pmb{\theta}}||_{2} \leq \frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sigma_{n}^{2}}$ . hence,

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {n} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i} \right] \right| \right| _ {2} \left| \left[ \boldsymbol {a} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {j} \left[ \boldsymbol {b} _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \right] _ {i j} \right| \leq \frac {n \sqrt {n} m _ {1} m _ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}}.
$$

combining these results and using  $||\nabla_{\pmb{\theta}}k_{\pmb{\theta}}(\pmb{x},\pmb{x})|| \leq m_2$ ,  $|\sigma_{\pmb{\theta}}(\pmb{x}|\mathcal{d})| \geq k_{\pmb{\theta}}(\pmb{x},\pmb{x}) \geq m_0$ , we have

$$
\left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \left[ \sigma_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} | \mathcal {d}) \right] \right| \right| _ {2} \leq \frac {n \sqrt {n} m _ {1} m _ {2}}{2 \sigma_ {n} ^ {2} m _ {0}} \left[ \frac {n ^ {2} \sqrt {p} m _ {1}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}} + 2 \right] \tag {14}
$$

hence, combining (13) and (14) we have

$$
\left| \left| h _ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} | \mathcal {d}) \right| \right| _ {2} \leq \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} \mu_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} | \mathcal {d}) \right| \right| _ {2} + \sqrt {\frac {\beta \pi}{2}} \left| \left| \nabla_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} [ \sigma_ {\boldsymbol {\theta}} (\boldsymbol {x} | \mathcal {d}) ] \right| \right| _ {2} \leq
$$

$$
\frac {(c + m _ {4}) n ^ {2} m _ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}} \left[ 1 + \frac {n ^ {2} m _ {1} \sqrt {p}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}} \right] + \sqrt {\frac {\beta \pi}{2}} \frac {n \sqrt {n} m _ {1} m _ {2}}{2 \sigma_ {n} ^ {2} m _ {0}} \left[ \frac {n ^ {2} \sqrt {p} m _ {1}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}} + 2 \right] \triangleq a _ {1}.
$$

now, we are ready to bound the other two terms in the claim:

$$
\mu_ {\pmb {\theta} + \pmb {\epsilon}} ^ {2} (\pmb {x} | \mathcal {d}) \leq 2 \left[ \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta} + \pmb {\epsilon}} ^ {\top} \pmb {b} _ {\pmb {\theta} + \pmb {\epsilon}} [ \pmb {y} - \pmb {m} _ {\mathcal {d}} ] \right] ^ {2} + 2 | m | ^ {2} \leq 2 \frac {(c + m _ {4}) ^ {2} m _ {1} ^ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}} + 2 m _ {4} ^ {2}
$$

therefore, for  $\mathbb{e}_{\epsilon}\left[\mu_{\pmb{\theta} + \epsilon}^{2}(\pmb{x}|\mathcal{d})\right]$  we have

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {\epsilon} \left[ \mu_ {\pmb {\theta} + \epsilon} ^ {2} (\pmb {x} | \mathcal {d}) \right] \leq 2 \frac {(c + m _ {4}) ^ {2} m _ {1} ^ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {4}} + 2 m _ {4} ^ {2} \triangleq a _ {2}.
$$

finally, for the posterior mean

$$
\sigma_ {\pmb {\theta} + \epsilon} ^ {2} (\pmb {x} | \mathcal {d}) \leq k _ {\pmb {\theta}} (\pmb {x}, \pmb {x}) + \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta} + \epsilon} ^ {\top} \pmb {b} _ {\pmb {\theta} + \epsilon} \pmb {a} _ {\pmb {\theta} + \epsilon} \leq m _ {1} + \frac {m _ {1} ^ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}}
$$

therefore, for  $\mathbb{e}_{\epsilon}\left[\sigma_{\pmb{\theta} + \pmb{\epsilon}}^{2}(\pmb{x}|\mathcal{d})\right]$  we have

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {\boldsymbol {\epsilon}} \left[ \sigma_ {\boldsymbol {\theta} + \boldsymbol {\epsilon}} ^ {2} (\boldsymbol {x} | \mathcal {d}) \right] \leq m _ {1} + \frac {m _ {1} ^ {2}}{\sigma_ {n} ^ {2}} \triangleq a _ {3}.
$$

this finishes the proof of the claim.

equipped with these results, we can further simplify the expressions (7):

$$
\sigma_ {n} = \frac {\rho}{4 \phi^ {- 1} \left(1 - \frac {\delta}{8 n _ {\epsilon}}\right)}, \sigma_ {\epsilon} = \min \left\{1, \frac {\rho}{8 \left[ 2 \sqrt {p} + \sqrt {\log \frac {4 n _ {\epsilon}}{\delta}} \right] [ a _ {1} + o (1) ]} \right\},
$$

with

$$
n _ {\epsilon} = \left\lceil \frac {1 6 \left[ a _ {2} + \frac {\beta \pi}{2} a _ {3} \right]}{\delta \rho^ {2}} \right\rceil .
$$

this finishes the proof of the lemma.

as such, we may now implement robust formulations of acquisition functions using only the gp predictive mean and variance.

# appendix d. task-level results breakdown

in this section we present the full task-level breakdown of the results with each metric, data set and model combination for each black-box optimiser summarised with the mean and variance achieved across 20 seeds. we show a summary plot in table 11.

we now present sequentially the full results for each of the 6 datasets: boston (section d.1), breast cancer (section d.2), diabetes (section d.3), digits (section d.4), iris (section d.5), wine (section d.6). for each optimiser we give the mean and variance of the performance metric across all 18 tasks (2 metrics x 9 models) for a given data set.
